CK3 ANTIQUITY TOTAL CONVERSION
359 BC TITLE RENAMING REFERENCE — PASS 1
=========================================

SOURCE INVENTORY
----------------
Counties: 3476
Duchies: 911 landed/de jure + 31 uncreatable = 942
Kingdoms: 215 de jure + 31 titular/creatable + 17 uncreatable = 263
Empires: 45 de jure + 34 titular/creatable = 79

Target date: 359 BC.

IMPORTANT METHOD NOTE
---------------------
CK3's territorial hierarchy is medieval. Many CK3 counties and duchies did not exist as named
administrative units in 359 BC, so a literal one-to-one historical name often does not exist.
This file therefore uses the best available ancient toponym, people/tribe, polity, or geographic
region for the same area. When no secure local one-to-one equivalent is available, the ancient
regional equivalent is used and marked [R]. Repeated [R] names are deliberate flags that the
underlying medieval title layout should eventually be redrawn or subdivided rather than treated
as proof that an ancient county of that exact shape existed.

Tags:
[A/R] = direct ancient equivalent or a close localized historical reconstruction.
[R]   = broader regional / ethnogeographic 359 BC fallback.
[K]   = intentionally retained because it is already suitable, or because East/Southeast Asia,
        sub-Saharan Africa, Tibet, or another already-appropriate indigenous name was left alone.
[M]   = structural merge or title that should be removed rather than survive as a separate
        359 BC territorial title.

Specific project decisions reflected here:
- France + Aquitaine + Brittany -> GAUL at kingdom level.
- Francia is folded into the Gaul structure rather than treated as a medieval Frankish empire.
- Iberian medieval kingdoms are replaced with ancient Iberian ethnogeographic regions.
- Medieval/Islamic MENA naming is replaced heavily with pre-Hellenistic, Punic, Egyptian,
  Iranian, Mesopotamian, Levantine, North African, and South Arabian equivalents.
- Obvious post-359 BC foundations are replaced when possible. Examples include:
  Alexandria -> Rhakotis; Thessalonika -> Therma; Nikaea -> Bithynia; Antioch -> Lower Orontes.
- CK3 1.19 already calls the Constantinople county BYZANTION, so it remains Byzantion here.
- East Asia and sub-Saharan Africa are intentionally much less altered, as requested.

Line format:
ORIGINAL CK3 NAME -> 359 BC RECOMMENDATION [TAG] {source category} ; note/context
